BFFS16 if \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le (\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+})^3 (\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+})^3 + (\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-})^3 (\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-})^3 \le 1$$\end{document}.

Bq-ROFS15 if \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le (\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+})^{n} + (\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+})^{m} \le 1$$\end{document}, and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le |\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}|^{n} + |\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}|^{m} \le 1$$\end{document}, where \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$n = m = q$$\end{document}.

Bq-ROFS15 if \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le (\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+})^{n} + (\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+})^{m} \le 1$$\end{document}, and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le |\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}|^{n} + |\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}|^{m} \le 1$$\end{document}, where \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$n = m = q$$\end{document}.

Definition 2.3

18 Let \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {U}$$\end{document} be a universal set. Then,\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}=\left\{ \left\langle \nu , \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ) +i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ),\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ) +i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ), \right. \right.$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left. \left. \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu ) +i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu ), \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu ) +i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu )\right\rangle : \nu \in \mathscr {U} \right\}$$\end{document}is called a BCIFS if\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ) + \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ) \le 1,\quad 0 \le \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ) + \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ) \le 1;$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le |\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu )|+ |\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu )|\le 1,\quad 0 \le |\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu )|+ |\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu )|\le 1,$$\end{document}where\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ),\, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ), \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ),\, \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu )&\in [0,1], \end{aligned}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu ),\, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu ), \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu ),\, \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu )&\in [-1,0]. \end{aligned}$$\end{document}

Bipolar complex q-rung orthopair fuzzy sets

Bipolar fuzzy sets extend classical fuzzy sets by assigning each element both a positive membership degree, representing acceptance or satisfaction, and a negative membership degree, representing rejection or opposition. This dual evaluation allows for a realistic representation of situations where favorable and unfavorable aspects coexist, reflecting the nuances of human judgment and complex decision-making. When combined with complex numbers and the q-rung orthopair structure, bipolar fuzzy sets can model both real and imaginary components, as well as multidimensional uncertainty and conflicting preferences.

This section then presents a detailed description of the fundamental concepts and essential operations related to bipolar complex q-ROFS.

Definition 3.1

In the universe of discourse \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {U}$$\end{document}, a bipolar complex q-rung orthopair fuzzy set (BCq-ROFS) \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}$$\end{document} is characterized as follows:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}=\left\{ \left\langle \nu , \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ) +i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ),\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ) +i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ), \right. \right.$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left. \left. \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu ) +i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu ), \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu ) +i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu )\right\rangle : \nu \in \mathscr {U} \right\}$$\end{document}where\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ),\, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu )&\in [0,1] \quad \text {(real positive components)},\\ \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ),\, \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu )&\in [0,1] \quad \text {(imaginary positive components)}, \end{aligned}$$\end{document}with\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le (\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ))^{q} + (\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ))^{q} \le 1,\quad 0 \le (\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ))^{q} + (\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}(\nu ))^{q} \le 1;$$\end{document}and\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu ),\, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu )&\in [-1,0] \quad \text {(real negative components)},\\ \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu ),\, \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu )&\in [-1,0] \quad \text {(imaginary negative components)}, \end{aligned}$$\end{document}with\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le |\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu )|^{q} + |\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu )|^{q} \le 1,\quad 0 \le |\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu )|^{q} + |\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}(\nu )|^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}The value of \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\nu$$\end{document} is computed as\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H} = \langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \rangle$$\end{document}which represents the BCq-ROF number (BCq-ROFN).

Figure 1 illustrates the feasible space of bipolar complex q-rung orthopair fuzzy values, showing the interactions between the real and imaginary components for both positive and negative memberships. The figure highlights the admissible regions for different q values, with blue representing positive components and red representing negative components. This visualization clarifies the distinct behaviors and constraints of the bipolar complex components, providing a clear understanding of their relationships and how the q-rung structure shapes the feasible space.Fig. 1Grades space of BCq-ROF values for \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$q=1,2,3,4$$\end{document}.

Grades space of BCq-ROF values for \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$q=1,2,3,4$$\end{document}.

Example 3.2

Consider a decision-making scenario in which a company evaluates two renewable energy projects, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {P}_1$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {P}_2$$\end{document}, based on two attributes: economic viability (\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr{A}\mathscr{T}_1$$\end{document}) and environmental impact (\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr{A}\mathscr{T}_2$$\end{document}). The evaluations are expressed using BC2-ROFSs as follows:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} {\mathcal{H}}_{{11}} = & \langle 0.8 + 0.6i,0.2 + 0.1i, - 0.3 - 0.2i, - 0.1 - 0.05i\rangle , \\ {\mathcal{H}}_{{12}} = & \langle 0.7 + 0.5i,0.3 + 0.1i, - 0.2 - 0.1i, - 0.05 - 0.02i\rangle , \\ {\mathcal{H}}_{{21}} = & \langle 0.6 + 0.4i,0.3 + 0.2i, - 0.1 - 0.05i, - 0.2 - 0.1i\rangle , \\ {\mathcal{H}}_{{22}} = & \langle 0.9 + 0.7i,0.1 + 0.05i, - 0.4 - 0.3i, - 0.1 - 0.02i\rangle . \\ \end{aligned}$$\end{document}Here:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {M}^+ + i \mathscr {A}^+$$\end{document}: positive membership (supporting evidence),\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {N}^+ + i \mathscr {B}^+$$\end{document}: positive non-membership (degree of not supporting),\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {M}^- + i \mathscr {A}^-$$\end{document}: negative membership (opposing evidence),\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {N}^- + i \mathscr {B}^-$$\end{document}: negative non-membership (degree of not opposing).

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {M}^+ + i \mathscr {A}^+$$\end{document}: positive membership (supporting evidence),

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {M}^+ + i \mathscr {A}^+$$\end{document}: positive membership (supporting evidence),

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {N}^+ + i \mathscr {B}^+$$\end{document}: positive non-membership (degree of not supporting),

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {N}^+ + i \mathscr {B}^+$$\end{document}: positive non-membership (degree of not supporting),

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {M}^- + i \mathscr {A}^-$$\end{document}: negative membership (opposing evidence),

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {M}^- + i \mathscr {A}^-$$\end{document}: negative membership (opposing evidence),

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {N}^- + i \mathscr {B}^-$$\end{document}: negative non-membership (degree of not opposing).

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {N}^- + i \mathscr {B}^-$$\end{document}: negative non-membership (degree of not opposing).

Definition 3.3

For any two BCq-ROFNs, let the following be defined:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 = \left\langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right\rangle$$\end{document}and\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_2 = \left\langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right\rangle .$$\end{document}We have:

1. \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 \subseteq \mathcal {H}_2$$\end{document} if and only if \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \le \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \quad \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \ge \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}, \quad \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \ge \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \quad \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \le \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}$$\end{document} for real terms and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \le \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \quad \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \ge \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}, \quad \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \ge \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \quad \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \le \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}$$\end{document} for imaginary terms.

2. \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 = \mathcal {H}_2$$\end{document} if and only if \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} = \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \quad \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} = \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}, \quad \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} = \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \quad \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} = \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-},$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} = \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \quad \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} = \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}, \quad \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} = \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \quad \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} = \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}.$$\end{document}

3. 1\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} {\mathcal {H}_1}^{c}=\begin{array}{c}\langle \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} +i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+},\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} +i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, -|\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}| +i (-|\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}|) , -|\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}| +i (-|\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}|)\rangle . \end{array} \end{aligned}$$\end{document}

4. 2\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} \mathcal {H}_1 \cap \mathcal {H}_2= \begin{array}{c} \langle \min \{\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\} +i \min \{\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\}, \\ \max \{\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\} +i \max \{\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\},\\ \max \{\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}\} +i \max \{\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}, \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}\}, \\ \min \{\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}\} +i \min \{\mathscr {B}_ {\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}, \mathscr {B}_ {\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}\} \rangle . \end{array} \end{aligned}$$\end{document}

5. 3\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} \mathcal {H}_1 \cup \mathcal {H}_2=\begin{array}{c}\langle \max \{\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\} +i \max \{\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {A} _{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\}, \\ \min \{\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\} +i \min \{\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {B}_ {\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\}, \\ \min \{\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}\} +i \min \{\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}, \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}\}, \\ \max \{\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}\} +i \max \{\mathscr {B}_ {\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}, \mathscr {B}_ {\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}\} \rangle . \end{array} \end{aligned}$$\end{document}

Example 3.4

Define the set \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {U}=\left\{ \nu _{1},\nu _{2}, \nu _{3}\right\}$$\end{document}. To enhance conciseness and maintain consistency in numerical expressions, all decimal values within the open interval \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$(-1,1)$$\end{document} are represented without a leading zero. Then, the set \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1$$\end{document} is expressed as:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1=\left\{ \begin{array}{c}\left\langle \nu _1, .8+(.2)i,.7+(.1)i,-.2+(-.2)i,-.7+(-.1)i\right\rangle , \\ \left\langle \nu _2, .4+(.1)i,.6+(.2)i,-.1+(-.1)i,-.2+(-.3)i\right\rangle , \\ \left\langle \nu _3, .3+(.3)i,.8+(.2)i,-.6+(-.1)i,-.5+(-.1)i\right\rangle \end{array}\right\}$$\end{document}

Similarly, the set \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_2$$\end{document} is given by:

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_2=$$\end{document} \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left\{ \begin{array}{c}\left\langle \nu _1, .7+(.1)i,.8+(.2)i,-.7+(-.3)i,-.2+(-.5)i\right\rangle , \\ \left\langle \nu _2, .6+(.2)i,.4+(.1)i,-.2+(-.5)i,-.1+(-.3)i\right\rangle , \\ \left\langle \nu _3, .6+(.1)i,.5+(.2)i,-.8+(-.3)i,-.3+(-.5)i\right\rangle \end{array}\right\}$$\end{document}.

Both \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_2$$\end{document} represent BC3-ROFNs. Consequently, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_{1}^{c}=\left\{ \begin{array}{c}\left\langle \nu _1, .7+(.1)i,.8+(.2)i,-.7+(-.1)i,-.2+(-.2)i\right\rangle , \\ \left\langle \nu _2, .6+(.2)i,.4+(.1)i,-.2+(-.3)i,-.1+(-.1)i\right\rangle , \\ \left\langle \nu _3, .8+(.2)i,.3+(.3)i,-.5+(-.1)i,-.6+(-.1)i\right\rangle \end{array}\right\}$$\end{document}.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 \cap \mathcal {H}_2= \left\{ \begin{array}{c}\left\langle \nu _1, .7+(.1)i,.8+(.2)i,-.2+(-.2)i,-.7+(-.5)i\right\rangle \\ \left\langle \nu _2, .4+(.1)i,.6+(.2)i,-.1+(-.1)i,-.2+(-.3)i\right\rangle \\ \left\langle \nu _3, .3+(.1)i,.8+(.2)i,-.6+(-.1)i,-.5+(-.5)i\right\rangle \end{array}\right\}$$\end{document}.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 \cup \mathcal {H}_2=\left\{ \begin{array}{c}\left\langle \nu _1, .8+(.2)i,.7+(.1)i,-.7+(-.3)i,-.2+(-.1)i\right\rangle \\ \left\langle \nu _2, .6+(.2)i,.4+(.1)i,-.2+(-.5)i,-.1+(-.3)i\right\rangle \\ \left\langle \nu _3, .6+(.3)i,.5+(.2)i,-.8+(-.3)i,-.3+(-.1)i \right\rangle \end{array}\right\}$$\end{document}.

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_{1}^{c}=\left\{ \begin{array}{c}\left\langle \nu _1, .7+(.1)i,.8+(.2)i,-.7+(-.1)i,-.2+(-.2)i\right\rangle , \\ \left\langle \nu _2, .6+(.2)i,.4+(.1)i,-.2+(-.3)i,-.1+(-.1)i\right\rangle , \\ \left\langle \nu _3, .8+(.2)i,.3+(.3)i,-.5+(-.1)i,-.6+(-.1)i\right\rangle \end{array}\right\}$$\end{document}.

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_{1}^{c}=\left\{ \begin{array}{c}\left\langle \nu _1, .7+(.1)i,.8+(.2)i,-.7+(-.1)i,-.2+(-.2)i\right\rangle , \\ \left\langle \nu _2, .6+(.2)i,.4+(.1)i,-.2+(-.3)i,-.1+(-.1)i\right\rangle , \\ \left\langle \nu _3, .8+(.2)i,.3+(.3)i,-.5+(-.1)i,-.6+(-.1)i\right\rangle \end{array}\right\}$$\end{document}.

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 \cap \mathcal {H}_2= \left\{ \begin{array}{c}\left\langle \nu _1, .7+(.1)i,.8+(.2)i,-.2+(-.2)i,-.7+(-.5)i\right\rangle \\ \left\langle \nu _2, .4+(.1)i,.6+(.2)i,-.1+(-.1)i,-.2+(-.3)i\right\rangle \\ \left\langle \nu _3, .3+(.1)i,.8+(.2)i,-.6+(-.1)i,-.5+(-.5)i\right\rangle \end{array}\right\}$$\end{document}.

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 \cap \mathcal {H}_2= \left\{ \begin{array}{c}\left\langle \nu _1, .7+(.1)i,.8+(.2)i,-.2+(-.2)i,-.7+(-.5)i\right\rangle \\ \left\langle \nu _2, .4+(.1)i,.6+(.2)i,-.1+(-.1)i,-.2+(-.3)i\right\rangle \\ \left\langle \nu _3, .3+(.1)i,.8+(.2)i,-.6+(-.1)i,-.5+(-.5)i\right\rangle \end{array}\right\}$$\end{document}.

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 \cup \mathcal {H}_2=\left\{ \begin{array}{c}\left\langle \nu _1, .8+(.2)i,.7+(.1)i,-.7+(-.3)i,-.2+(-.1)i\right\rangle \\ \left\langle \nu _2, .6+(.2)i,.4+(.1)i,-.2+(-.5)i,-.1+(-.3)i\right\rangle \\ \left\langle \nu _3, .6+(.3)i,.5+(.2)i,-.8+(-.3)i,-.3+(-.1)i \right\rangle \end{array}\right\}$$\end{document}.

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 \cup \mathcal {H}_2=\left\{ \begin{array}{c}\left\langle \nu _1, .8+(.2)i,.7+(.1)i,-.7+(-.3)i,-.2+(-.1)i\right\rangle \\ \left\langle \nu _2, .6+(.2)i,.4+(.1)i,-.2+(-.5)i,-.1+(-.3)i\right\rangle \\ \left\langle \nu _3, .6+(.3)i,.5+(.2)i,-.8+(-.3)i,-.3+(-.1)i \right\rangle \end{array}\right\}$$\end{document}.

Theorem 3.5

Let\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}=\left\langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}+i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} +i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} +i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} +i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}\right\rangle$$\end{document}be a BCq-ROFN. Similarly, define\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 = \left\langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_{1}}^{+}+i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_{1}}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_{1}}^{+} +i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_{1}}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_{1}}^{-} +i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_{1}}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_{1}}^{-} +i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_{1}}^{-}\right\rangle$$\end{document}and\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_2 = \left\langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_{2}}^{+}+i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_{2}}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_{2}}^{+} +i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_{2}}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_{2}}^{-} +i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_{2}}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_{2}}^{-} +i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_{2}}^{-}\right\rangle .$$\end{document}These represent BCq-ROFNs, and therefore,

1. \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathcal {H}}^{c}$$\end{document} is a BCq-ROFN, and applying the complement twice yields \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$({\mathcal {H}}^{c})^{c}=\mathcal {H}$$\end{document}.

2.

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_{1}\cup \mathcal {H}_{2}$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_{1}\cap \mathcal {H}_{2}$$\end{document} are also BCq-ROFN.

Proof

1. Since \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{gathered} 0 \le ({\mathscr{M}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ + } )^{q} + ({\mathscr{N}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ + } )^{q} \le 1, \hfill \\ 0 \le ({\mathscr{A}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ + } )^{q} + ({\mathscr{B}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ + } )^{q} \le 1, \hfill \\ 0 \le \left| {{\mathscr{M}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ - } } \right|^{q} + \left| {{\mathscr{N}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ - } } \right|^{q} \le 1{\mkern 1mu} {\mkern 1mu} {\mkern 1mu} {\mkern 1mu} {\mkern 1mu} {\mkern 1mu} {\mkern 1mu} \hfill \\ \,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,and \hfill \\ 0 \le |{\mathscr{A}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ - } |^{q} + |{\mathscr{B}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ - } |^{q} \le 1 \hfill \\ \end{gathered}$$\end{document} then \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{gathered} 0 \le ({\mathscr{N}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ + } )^{q} + ({\mathscr{M}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ + } )^{q} = ({\mathscr{M}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ + } )^{q} + ({\mathscr{N}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ + } )^{q} \le 1, \hfill \\ 0 \le ({\mathscr{B}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ + } )^{q} + ({\mathscr{A}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ + } )^{q} = ({\mathscr{A}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ + } )^{q} + ({\mathscr{B}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ + } )^{q} \le 1, \hfill \\ 0 \le \left| { - \left| {{\mathscr{N}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ - } } \right|} \right|^{q} + \left| { - \left| {{\mathscr{M}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ - } } \right|} \right|^{q} = \left| {{\mathscr{M}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ - } } \right|^{q} + \left| {{\mathscr{N}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ - } } \right|^{q} \le 1 \hfill \\ \,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,{\mathrm{and}} \hfill \\ 0 \le \left| { - \left| {{\mathscr{B}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ - } } \right|} \right|^{q} + \left| { - \left| {{\mathscr{A}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ - } } \right|} \right|^{q} = |{\mathscr{A}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ - } |^{q} + |{\mathscr{B}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ - } |^{q} \le 1. \hfill \\ \end{gathered}$$\end{document}Hence, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}^{c}$$\end{document} is a BCq-ROFN, and it is evident that \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{gathered} ({\mathcal {H}}^{c})^{c}=\langle \mathscr {N}_\mathcal {H}^{+} +i \mathscr {B}_\mathcal {H}^{+},\mathscr {M}_\mathcal {H}^{+} +i \mathscr {A}_\mathcal {H}^{+}, -|\mathscr {N}_\mathcal {H}^{-}|+i (-|\mathscr {B}_\mathcal {H}^{-}|), -|\mathscr {M}_\mathcal {H}^{-}| +i (-|\mathscr {A}_\mathcal {H}^{-}|)\rangle ^{c}\\ = \langle \mathscr {M}_\mathcal {H}^{+} +i \mathscr {A}_\mathcal {H}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_\mathcal {H}^{+} +i \mathscr {B}_\mathcal {H}^{+}, -| -|\mathscr {M}_\mathcal {H}^{-}|| +i (-|-|\mathscr {A}_\mathcal {H}^{-}||), -|-|\mathscr {N}_\mathcal {H}^{-}|| +i (-|-|\mathscr {B}_\mathcal {H}^{-}||)\rangle \\ =\langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} +i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+},\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} +i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} +i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} +i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}\rangle, \end{gathered}$$\end{document} where \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}= -\left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}\right| ,\quad \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}=-\left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}\right|$$\end{document}, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}= -\left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}\right|$$\end{document}, and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\quad \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}=-\left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}\right|$$\end{document}.

2.

Since the following inequalities hold: \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+},\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le |\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}|, |\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}|, |\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}|, |\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}|, |\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}|,|\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}|, |\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}|,|\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}| \le 1,$$\end{document} it is evident that the following conditions hold: \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \max \left\{ \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right\} \right) ^q + \left( \min \left\{ \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right\} \right) ^q \le 1,$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \max \left\{ \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right\} \right) ^q + \left( \min \left\{ \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right\} \right) ^q \le 1,$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \min \left\{ |\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}|, |\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}| \right\} \right) ^q + \left( \max \left\{ |\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}|, |\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}| \right\} \right) ^q \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \max \left\{ |\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}|, |\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}| \right\} \right) ^q + \left( \min \left\{ |\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}|, |\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}| \right\} \right) ^q \le 1.$$\end{document} Therefore, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 \cup \mathcal {H}_2$$\end{document} is a BCq-ROFN. A similar proof can be applied to show that \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 \cap \mathcal {H}_2$$\end{document} is also a BCq-ROFN.

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\square$$\end{document}

Theorem 3.6

Let \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathcal{H}}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\langle {\mathscr{M}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ + }$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$+ i{\mathscr{A}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ + }$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$,{\mathscr{N}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ + }$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$+ i{\mathscr{B}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ + }$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$,{\mathscr{M}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ - }$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$+ i{\mathscr{A}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ - } ,$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathscr{N}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ - }$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$+ i{\mathscr{B}}_{{\mathcal{H}}}^{ - } \rangle$$\end{document}, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathcal{H}}_{1}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\langle {\mathscr{M}}_{{{\mathcal{H}}_{1} }}^{ + }$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$+ i{\mathscr{A}}_{{{\mathcal{H}}_{1} }}^{ + }$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$,{\mathscr{N}}_{{{\mathcal{H}}_{1} }}^{ + }$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$+ i{\mathscr{B}}_{{{\mathcal{H}}_{1} }}^{ + }$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$,{\mathscr{M}}_{{{\mathcal{H}}_{1} }}^{ - }$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$+ i{\mathscr{A}}_{{{\mathcal{H}}_{1} }}^{ - } ,$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathscr{N}}_{{{\mathcal{H}}_{1} }}^{ - }$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$+ i{\mathscr{B}}_{{{\mathcal{H}}_{1} }}^{ - } \rangle$$\end{document}, and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_2 = \langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \rangle$$\end{document} be BCq-ROFNs. Then the following properties hold: \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_2 \cap \mathcal {H}_1=\mathcal {H}_1 \cap \mathcal {H}_2$$\end{document}.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_2 \cup \mathcal {H}_1=\mathcal {H}_1 \cup \mathcal {H}_2$$\end{document}.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_2=(\mathcal {H}_1 \cup \mathcal {H}_2) \cap \mathcal {H}_2$$\end{document}.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} Proof

Therefore, the results are obvious. \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\square$$\end{document}

Theorem 3.7

Let \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 = \langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \rangle$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_2$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \rangle$$\end{document} be BCq-ROFNs. Then, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathcal {H}_1}^{c}\cup {\mathcal {H}_2}^{c}={\left( \mathcal {H}_1\cap \mathcal {H}_2\right) }^{c}$$\end{document}.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathcal {H}_1}^{c}\cap {\mathcal {H}_2}^{c}=\left( \mathcal {H}_1\cup \mathcal {H}_2\right) ^{c}$$\end{document}.

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathcal {H}_1}^{c}\cup {\mathcal {H}_2}^{c}={\left( \mathcal {H}_1\cap \mathcal {H}_2\right) }^{c}$$\end{document}.

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathcal {H}_1}^{c}\cup {\mathcal {H}_2}^{c}={\left( \mathcal {H}_1\cap \mathcal {H}_2\right) }^{c}$$\end{document}.

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathcal {H}_1}^{c}\cap {\mathcal {H}_2}^{c}=\left( \mathcal {H}_1\cup \mathcal {H}_2\right) ^{c}$$\end{document}.

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathcal {H}_1}^{c}\cap {\mathcal {H}_2}^{c}=\left( \mathcal {H}_1\cup \mathcal {H}_2\right) ^{c}$$\end{document}.

Proof

1. \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned}{\left( \mathcal {H}_1\cap \mathcal {H}_2\right) }^{c} & =\left\{ \begin{array}{c}\langle \{\min \left\{ \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right\} +i \min \left\{ \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right\} ,\\ \max \left\{ \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right\} +i \max \left\{ \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right\} ,\\ \max \left\{ \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}\right\} +i \max \left\{ \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}, \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}\right\} ,\\ \min \left\{ \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\} +i \max \{\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\},\\ \min \{\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\} +i \min \{\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \},\\ \min \{-|\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}|, -|\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}|\} +i \min \{-|\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}|, -|\mathscr {B}_ {\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}|\},\\ \max \{-|\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}|, -|\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}|\} +i \max \{-|\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}|,-|\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_{2}}^{-}|\}\rangle \end{array}\right\} \\ & = \langle ({\mathcal{N}}_{{{\mathcal{H}}_{1} }}^{ + } ) + i({\mathcal{B}}_{{{\mathcal{H}}_{1} }}^{ + |{\mathcal{N}}_{{{\mathcal{H}}_{2} }}^{ - } | + i( - |{\mathcal{B}}_{{{\mathcal{H}}_{2} }}^{ - } |), - |{\mathcal{M}}_{{{\mathcal{H}}_{2} }}^{ - } | + i( - |{\mathcal{A}}_{{{\mathcal{H}}_{2} }}^{ - } |)\rangle \\ & = {\mathcal{H}}_{1} ^{c} \cup {\mathcal{H}}_{2} ^{c} = {\mathcal{H}}_{1} ^{c} \cup {\mathcal{H}}_{2} ^{c} .\end{aligned}$$\end{document}

2. This can be proven similarly to (1).

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} Example 3.9

We define two BC6-ROFNs as follows:

((.84)^{6} + (.55)^{6} - ((.84)^{6} (.55)^{6}))^{\frac{1}{6}} + i ((.73)^{6} + (.84)^{6} - ((.73)^{6} (.84)^{6}))^{\frac{1}{6}}, \right.$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left. - (|-.55|^{6} + |-.92|^{6} - |-.55|^{6} |-.92|^{6})^{\frac{1}{6}} \right.$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left. + i (-(|-.92|^{6} + |-.73|^{6} - | -.92|^{6} |-.73|^{6})^{\frac{1}{6}}), \right.$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left. - ((-.84) (-.73)) + i (-((-.73) (-.92))) \right\rangle$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\approx \langle .3404+.7728 i,.8470+.8752 i, -.9227+(-.9345)i,-.6132+(-.6716)i\rangle$$\end{document}.

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 \otimes \mathcal {H}_2=$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left\langle \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) + i \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) , \right.$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left. \left( \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} -
