Theorem 3.10

Consider two BCq-ROFNs \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_2$$\end{document} defined as follows:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 = \left\langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right\rangle$$\end{document}and\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_2 = \left\langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right\rangle .$$\end{document}Then, it follows that both \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 \oplus \mathcal {H}_2$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 \otimes \mathcal {H}_2$$\end{document} are also BCq-ROFNs.

Proof

For the two BCq-ROFNs, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_2$$\end{document}, the following inequalities hold:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} &0 \le \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \le 1, \\& 0 \le \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}\right| ^{q} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}\right| ^{q} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}\right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}\right| ^{q} \le 1, \\& 0 \le \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \le 1, \\& 0 \le \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}\right| ^{q} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}\right| ^{q} \le 1, \quad \text {and} \quad 0 \le \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}\right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}\right| ^{q} \le 1. \end{aligned}$$\end{document}Moreover, given that:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned}&0 \le \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \le 1, \\& 0 \le (\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+})^{q} + (\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+})^{q} \le 1, \quad 0 \le (\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+})^{q} + (\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+})^{q} \le 1, \\& 0 \le |\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}| \le 1, \quad 0 \le |\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}| \le 1, \quad 0 \le |\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}| \le 1, \quad 0 \le |\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}| \le 1, \\& 0 \le |\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}|^{q} + |\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-}|^{q} \le 1, \quad \text {and} \quad 0 \le |\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}|^{q} + |\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-}|^{q} \le 1. \end{aligned}$$\end{document}These inequalities establish the necessary constraints on the parameters of the BCq-ROFNs. Then, we obtain the following inequalities:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned}&\left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \ge \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q}, \quad \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \ge \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q}, \\&\quad 1 \ge \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \ge 0, \quad \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \ge \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q}, \\&\quad \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \ge \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q}, \quad \text {and} \quad 1 \ge \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \ge . \end{aligned}$$\end{document}These relationships imply that:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \ge 0 \end{aligned}$$\end{document}which further leads to:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} \left( \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q}\right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \ge . \end{aligned}$$\end{document}Similarly, we have:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \ge 0, \end{aligned}$$\end{document}which implies:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} \left( \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q}\right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \ge . \end{aligned}$$\end{document}Given that\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \le 1 \quad \text {and} \quad 0 \le 1-\left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q},$$\end{document}we obtain the inequality\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( 1 - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \right) \le 1 - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q}.$$\end{document}From this, it follows that\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}Taking the \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$q$$\end{document}th root on both sides gives\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left( \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1.$$\end{document}By applying the same reasoning, we derive a similar bound:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left( \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1.$$\end{document}Furthermore, it is evident that\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \le 1 - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q}, \quad 0 \le \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \le 1 - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q}.$$\end{document}Using these bounds, we establish the inequality\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned}&\quad \left( \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} + \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} \\&\le \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} + \left( 1 - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \right) \left( 1 - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \right) = 1. \end{aligned}$$\end{document}Thus, we conclude that\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1,$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \le 1,$$\end{document}and\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}Similarly, we obtain the following inequalities:

1. \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned}&0 \le \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \le 1, \\&0 \le \left( \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1, \\ 0&\le \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} + \left( \left( \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+}\right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+}\right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right) ^{q} \le 1. \end{aligned}$$\end{document}

2.

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} -1&\le -\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \le 0, \\ -1&\le -\left( \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} - \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 0, \\ 0&\le \left| -\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| -\left( \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} - \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right| ^{q} \le 1. \end{aligned}$$\end{document}

3.

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} -1&\le -\left( \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} - \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 0, \\ -1&\le -\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \le 0, \\ 0&\le \left| -\left( \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} - \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right| ^{q} + \left| -\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} \le 1. \end{aligned}$$\end{document}

Following the same approach, we derive:(i) \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right) ^{q} + \left( \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) \right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}(ii) \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left( \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} - \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) \right| ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left( \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} - \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right| ^{q} + \left( \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| \right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}(iii) \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left( \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} - \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) \right| ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left( \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} - \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right| ^{q} + \left( \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| \right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}(iv) \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right) ^{q} + \left( \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) \right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}Therefore, it follows that \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 \oplus \mathcal {H}_2$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 \otimes \mathcal {H}_2$$\end{document} satisfy the conditions for being BCq-ROFNs. \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\square$$\end{document}

(i) \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right) ^{q} + \left( \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) \right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}

(i) \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right) ^{q} + \left( \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) \right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}

(ii) \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left( \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} - \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) \right| ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left( \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} - \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right| ^{q} + \left( \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| \right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}

(ii) \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left( \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} - \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) \right| ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left( \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} - \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right| ^{q} + \left( \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| \right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}

(iii) \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left( \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} - \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) \right| ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left( \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} - \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right| ^{q} + \left( \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| \right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}

(iii) \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left( \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} - \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) \right| ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left( \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} - \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| ^{q} \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right| ^{q} + \left( \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{-} \right| \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{-} \right| \right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}

(iv) \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right) ^{q} + \left( \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) \right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}

(iv) \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} - \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) ^{q} \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right) ^{q} + \left( \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+} \right) \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+} \right) \right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}

Theorem 3.11

Assume that \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H} = \langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \rangle$$\end{document} is a BCq-ROFN, and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {I}>0$$\end{document}. Then, both \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {I}\mathcal {H}$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}^{\mathscr {I}}$$\end{document} are also BCq-ROFNs.

Proof

We start by noting the following inequalities:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} + \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} \le 1,$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} + \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}This implies the following bounds:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} \le 1 - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q}, \quad 0 \le \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} \le 1 - \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q}.$$\end{document}Thus, we can conclude:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( 1 - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}}, \quad 0 \le \left( 1 - \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}}.$$\end{document}Next, we apply the following relations:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$1 - \left( 1 - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \le 1, \quad 1 - \left( 1 - \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \le 1.$$\end{document}This leads to the bounds:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1.$$\end{document}We also observe that:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \le 1, \quad -1 \le - \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{\mathscr {I}} \le .$$\end{document}From this, we deduce:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right) ^{q} + \left( \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{q} \le 1,$$\end{document}and\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{\mathscr {I}} \right| ^{q} + \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1.$$\end{document}Next, we consider similar relations for other terms: \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right) ^{q} + \left( \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{\mathscr {I}} \le 1, \quad -1 \le - \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 0,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{\mathscr {I}} \right| ^{q} + \left| - \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right| ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}Similarly, we obtain the following relations:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{q} + \left( \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left( \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right) ^{q} \le 1,$$\end{document}and\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left| \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right| ^{q} + \left| -\left| \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{\mathscr {I}} \right| ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}Finally, from the above, we conclude:

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right) ^{q} + \left( \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right) ^{q} + \left( \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{\mathscr {I}} \le 1, \quad -1 \le - \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 0,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{\mathscr {I}} \right| ^{q} + \left| - \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right| ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{\mathscr {I}} \le 1, \quad -1 \le - \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 0,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{\mathscr {I}} \right| ^{q} + \left| - \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right| ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}

1. \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \le 1, \quad 0 \le \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 1,$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left( \left( \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{q} + \left( \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left( \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} \right) ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right) ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}

2. \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$-1 \le - \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \le 0, \quad 0 \le \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{\mathscr {I}} \le 1,$$\end{document}

and

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$0 \le \left| - \left( 1 - \left( 1 - \left| \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{q} \right) ^{\mathscr {I}} \right) ^{\frac{1}{q}} \right| ^{q} + \left| - \left| \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right| ^{\mathscr {I}} \right| ^{q} \le 1.$$\end{document}

Thus, it follows that both \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {I} \mathcal {H}$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}^{\mathscr {I}}$$\end{document} are BCq-ROFNs. \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\square$$\end{document}

Theorem 3.12

Consider the BCq-ROFNs given by\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H} = \left\langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}}^{-} \right\rangle ,$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_1 = \left\langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_{1}}^{+} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_{1}}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_{1}}^{+} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_{1}}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_{1}}^{-} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_{1}}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_{1}}^{-} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_{1}}^{-} \right\rangle ,$$\end{document}and\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_2 = \left\langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_{2}}^{+} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_{2}}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_{2}}^{+} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_{2}}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_{2}}^{-} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_{2}}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_{2}}^{-} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_{2}}^{-} \right\rangle .$$\end{document}Then, the following fundamental properties hold: \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_{2} \oplus \mathcal {H}_{1}=\mathcal {H}_{1} \oplus \mathcal {H}_{2}.$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_{2} \otimes \mathcal {H}_{1}=\mathcal {H}_1 \otimes \mathcal {H}_{2}$$\end{document}.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H} \oplus (\mathcal {H}_{1} \oplus \mathcal {H}_{2})=(\mathcal {H} \oplus \mathcal {H}_{1}) \oplus \mathcal {H}_{2}$$\end{document}.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H} \otimes (\mathcal {H}_{1} \otimes \mathcal {H}_{2})=(\mathcal {H} \otimes \mathcal {H}_{1}) \otimes \mathcal {H}_{2}$$\end{document}.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H} \oplus (\mathcal {H}_{1} \cup \mathcal {H}_{2})=(\mathcal {H} \oplus \mathcal {H}_{1}) \cup (\mathcal {H} \oplus \mathcal {H}_{2})$$\end{document}.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H} \oplus (\mathcal {H}_{1} \cap \mathcal {H}_{2})=(\mathcal {H} \oplus \mathcal {H}_{1}) \cap (\mathcal {H} \oplus \mathcal {H}_{2})$$\end{document}.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H} \otimes (\mathcal {H}_{1} \cup \mathcal {H}_{2})=(\mathcal {H} \otimes \mathcal {H}_{1}) \cup (\mathcal {H} \otimes \mathcal {H}_{2})$$\end{document}.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H} \otimes (\mathcal {H}_{1} \cap \mathcal {H}_{2})=(\mathcal {H} \otimes \mathcal {H}_{1}) \cap (\mathcal {H} \otimes \mathcal {H}_{2})$$\end{document}.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} Proof

Only expressions (1) and (2) are explicitly illustrated at this stage; however, the subsequent components may be derived or expressed analogously.

(1)\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {I}\left( \mathcal {H}_1\oplus \mathcal {H}_2\right) =$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned}&\mathscr {I}\left\langle \begin{array}{c} ((\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_1}^{+})^{q}+(\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_2}^{+})^{q}-(\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal BCq-ROF aggregation operators

This part is dedicated to exploring the utilization of BCq-ROF weighted average and geometric aggregation operators for handling and analyzing data within the framework of BCq-ROFS. A comprehensive discussion is provided, highlighting the mathematical principles underlying these aggregation techniques and their significance in decision-making processes.

Definition 4.1

Let us consider a set of BCq-ROFNs, symbolized as:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_{i} = \left\{ \left\langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_{i}}^{+} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_{i}}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_{i}}^{+} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_{i}}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_{i}}^{-} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_{i}}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_{i}}^{-} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_{i}}^{-} \right\rangle \right\} , \quad \forall i \in \{1,2,\dots ,k\}$$\end{document}where each \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_i$$\end{document} represents an individual BCq-ROFN. Additionally, we define a weight vector \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {X}$$\end{document} associated with these elements, given by:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {X} = \left( \mathscr {X}_1, \mathscr {X}_2, \dots , \mathscr {X}_k\right) ^T$$\end{document}where each weight component satisfies \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {X}_i > 0$$\end{document} and collectively they sum up to unity, that is, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\sum _{i=1}^{k} \mathscr {X}_i = 1$$\end{document}. Based on this formulation, we introduce two fundamental aggregation operators: The BCq-ROF weighted averaging (BCq-ROFWA) operator is a mapping BCq-ROFWA\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$: \mathcal {H}^k \rightarrow \mathcal {H}$$\end{document} which aggregates the given BCq-ROFNs using a weighted averaging approach. It is mathematically expressed as: 8\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} BCq-ROFWA\left( \mathcal {H}_1, \mathcal {H}_2, \ldots , \mathcal {H}_k\right) =\bigoplus _{i=1}^k \mathscr {X}_i \mathcal {H}_i=\mathscr {X}_1 \mathcal {H}_1 \oplus \mathscr {X}_2 \mathcal {H}_2 \oplus \cdots \oplus \mathscr {X}_k \mathcal {H}_k. \end{aligned}$$\end{document}The BCq-ROF weighted geometric (BCq-ROFWG) operator provides an alternative aggregation mechanism by employing a weighted geometric approach. It is defined as a mapping BCq-ROFWG\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$: \mathcal {H}^k \rightarrow \mathcal {H}$$\end{document}, and its functional form is given by: 9\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} BCq-ROFWG\left( \mathcal {H}_1, \mathcal {H}_2, \ldots , \mathcal {H}_k\right) =\bigotimes _{i=1}^k \mathcal {H}_i^{\mathscr {X}_i}=\mathcal {H}_1^{\mathscr {X}_1} \otimes \mathcal {H}_2^{\mathscr {X}_2} \otimes \cdots \otimes \mathcal {H}_k^{\mathscr {X}_k}. \end{aligned}$$\end{document} This operation applies a weighted geometric combination of the given BCq-ROFNs, effectively capturing a different mode of aggregation compared to the averaging approach.

The BCq-ROF weighted averaging (BCq-ROFWA) operator is a mapping BCq-ROFWA\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$: \mathcal {H}^k \rightarrow \mathcal {H}$$\end{document} which aggregates the given BCq-ROFNs using a weighted averaging approach. It is mathematically expressed as: 8\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} BCq-ROFWA\left( \mathcal {H}_1, \mathcal {H}_2, \ldots , \mathcal {H}_k\right) =\bigoplus _{i=1}^k \mathscr {X}_i \mathcal {H}_i=\mathscr {X}_1 \mathcal {H}_1 \oplus \mathscr {X}_2 \mathcal {H}_2 \oplus \cdots \oplus \mathscr {X}_k \mathcal {H}_k. \end{aligned}$$\end{document}

The BCq-ROF weighted averaging (BCq-ROFWA) operator is a mapping BCq-ROFWA\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$: \mathcal {H}^k \rightarrow \mathcal {H}$$\end{document} which aggregates the given BCq-ROFNs using a weighted averaging approach. It is mathematically expressed as: 8\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} BCq-ROFWA\left( \mathcal {H}_1, \mathcal {H}_2, \ldots , \mathcal {H}_k\right) =\bigoplus _{i=1}^k \mathscr {X}_i \mathcal {H}_i=\mathscr {X}_1 \mathcal {H}_1 \oplus \mathscr {X}_2 \mathcal {H}_2 \oplus \cdots \oplus \mathscr {X}_k \mathcal {H}_k. \end{aligned}$$\end{document}

The BCq-ROF weighted geometric (BCq-ROFWG) operator provides an alternative aggregation mechanism by employing a weighted geometric approach. It is defined as a mapping BCq-ROFWG\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$: \mathcal {H}^k \rightarrow \mathcal {H}$$\end{document}, and its functional form is given by: 9\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} BCq-ROFWG\left( \mathcal {H}_1, \mathcal {H}_2, \ldots , \mathcal {H}_k\right) =\bigotimes _{i=1}^k \mathcal {H}_i^{\mathscr {X}_i}=\mathcal {H}_1^{\mathscr {X}_1} \otimes \mathcal {H}_2^{\mathscr {X}_2} \otimes \cdots \otimes \mathcal {H}_k^{\mathscr {X}_k}. \end{aligned}$$\end{document} This operation applies a weighted geometric combination of the given BCq-ROFNs, effectively capturing a different mode of aggregation compared to the averaging approach.

The BCq-ROF weighted geometric (BCq-ROFWG) operator provides an alternative aggregation mechanism by employing a weighted geometric approach. It is defined as a mapping BCq-ROFWG\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$: \mathcal {H}^k \rightarrow \mathcal {H}$$\end{document}, and its functional form is given by: 9\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} BCq-ROFWG\left( \mathcal {H}_1, \mathcal {H}_2, \ldots , \mathcal {H}_k\right) =\bigotimes _{i=1}^k \mathcal {H}_i^{\mathscr {X}_i}=\mathcal {H}_1^{\mathscr {X}_1} \otimes \mathcal {H}_2^{\mathscr {X}_2} \otimes \cdots \otimes \mathcal {H}_k^{\mathscr {X}_k}. \end{aligned}$$\end{document} This operation applies a weighted geometric combination of the given BCq-ROFNs, effectively capturing a different mode of aggregation compared to the averaging approach.

Theorem 4.2

Let \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_i$$\end{document} represent a collection of BCq-ROFNs, where each element is defined as:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {H}_{i} = \left\langle \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_{i}}^{+} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_{i}}^{+}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_{i}}^{+} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_{i}}^{+}, \mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_{i}}^{-} + i \mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_{i}}^{-}, \mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_{i}}^{-} + i \mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_{i}}^{-} \right\rangle , \quad i=1,2,\dots ,k.$$\end{document}Furthermore, let \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {X} = \left( \mathscr {X}_1, \mathscr {X}_2, \dots , \mathscr {X}_k \right) ^T$$\end{document} be the corresponding weight vector, where each weight satisfies \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathscr {X}_i > 0$$\end{document} and the sum constraint \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\sum _{i=1}^{k} \mathscr {X}_i = 1$$\end{document}. Then, the BCq-ROFW averaging and geometric aggregation operators can be alternatively formulated as follows: The BCq-ROFWA operator aggregates the given BCq-ROFNs based on a weighted averaging approach, and its alternative formulation is: 10\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} \operatorname {BCq-ROFWA}\left( \mathcal {H}_1, \mathcal {H}_2, \ldots , \mathcal {H}_k\right) =\left\langle \begin{array}{c} (1-{\prod }_{i=1}^k(1-(\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{+})^{q})^{\mathscr {X}_i})^{\frac{1}{q}}+i(1-{\prod }_{i=1}^k(1-(\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{+})^{q})^{\mathscr {X}_i})^{\frac{1}{q}}, \\ {\prod }_{i=1}^k(\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{+})^{\mathscr {X}_i}+i{\prod }_{i=1}^k(\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{+})^{\mathscr {X}_i},\\ -({\prod }_{i=1}^k|\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{-}|^{\mathscr {X}_i})+i(-({\prod }_{i=1}^k|\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{-}|^{\mathscr {X}_i})) \\ -(1-{\prod }_{i=1}^k(1-|\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{-}|^{q})^{\mathscr {X}_i})^{\frac{1}{q}}+i(-(1-{\prod }_{i=1}^k(1-|\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{-}|^{q})^{\mathscr {X}_i})^{\frac{1}{q}})\end{array}\right\rangle \end{aligned}$$\end{document}The BCq-ROFWG operator follows a geometric-based aggregation method, and its alternative formulation is given by: 11\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} \operatorname {BCq-ROFWG}\left( \mathcal {H}_1, \mathcal {H}_2, \ldots , \mathcal {H}_k\right) = \left\langle \begin{array}{c}{\prod }_{i=1}^k(\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{+})^{\mathscr {X}_i}+i({\prod }_{i=1}^k\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{+})^{\mathscr {X}_{i}},\\ (1-{\prod }_{i=1}^k(1-(\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{+})^{q})^{\mathscr {X}_i})^{\frac{1}{q}}+i(1-{\prod }_{i=1}^k(1-(\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{+})^{q})^{\mathscr {X}_i})^{\frac{1}{q}}, \\ -(1-{\prod }_{i=1}^k(1-|\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{-}|^{q})^{\mathscr {X}_i})^{\frac{1}{q}}+i (-(1-{\prod }_{i=1}^k(1-|\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{-}|^{q})^{\mathscr {X}_i})^{\frac{1}{q}}),\\ -({\prod }_{i=1}^k|\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{-}|_i^{\mathscr {X}_i})+i(-({\prod }_{i=1}^k|\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{-}|_i^{\mathscr {X}_i}))\end{array}\right\rangle \end{aligned}$$\end{document}

The BCq-ROFWA operator aggregates the given BCq-ROFNs based on a weighted averaging approach, and its alternative formulation is: 10\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\begin{aligned} \operatorname {BCq-ROFWA}\left( \mathcal {H}_1, \mathcal {H}_2, \ldots , \mathcal {H}_k\right) =\left\langle \begin{array}{c} (1-{\prod }_{i=1}^k(1-(\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{+})^{q})^{\mathscr {X}_i})^{\frac{1}{q}}+i(1-{\prod }_{i=1}^k(1-(\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{+})^{q})^{\mathscr {X}_i})^{\frac{1}{q}}, \\ {\prod }_{i=1}^k(\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{+})^{\mathscr {X}_i}+i{\prod }_{i=1}^k(\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{+})^{\mathscr {X}_i},\\ -({\prod }_{i=1}^k|\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{-}|^{\mathscr {X}_i})+i(-({\prod }_{i=1}^k|\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{-}|^{\mathscr {X}_i})) \\ -(1-{\prod }_{i=1}^k(1-|\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{-}|^{q})^{\mathscr {X}_i})^{\frac{1}{q}}+i(-(1-{\prod }_{i=1}^k(1-|\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{-}|^{q})^{\mathscr {X}_i})^{\frac{1}{q}})\end{array}\right\rangle \end{aligned}$$\end{document}

In particular, we assume: \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\operatorname {BCq-ROFWA}(\mathcal {H}_1, \mathcal {H}_2, \ldots , \mathcal {H}_l)= \mathscr {X}_1 \mathcal {H}_1 \oplus \mathscr {X}_2 \mathcal {H}_2 \oplus \cdots \oplus \mathscr {X}_l \mathcal {H}_l$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left\langle \begin{array}{c}( 1-{\prod }_{i=1}^l(1- (\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{+})^{q})^{\mathscr {X}_i})^{\frac{1}{q}}+i( 1-{\prod }_{i=1}^l(1- (\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{+})^{q})^{\mathscr {X}_i})^{\frac{1}{q}},\\ {\prod }_{i=1}^l(\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{+})^{\mathscr {X}_i}+i{\prod }_{i=1}^l(\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{+})^{\mathscr {X}_i},-({\prod }_{i=1}^l|\mathscr {M}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{-}|^{\mathscr {X}_i})+i(-({\prod }_{i=1}^l|\mathscr {A}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{-}|^{\mathscr {X}_i})),\\ -(1- {\prod }_{i=1}^l(1-|\mathscr {N}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{-}|^{q})^{\mathscr {X}_i})^{\frac{1}{q}}+i(-(1- {\prod }_{i=1}^l(1-|\mathscr {B}_{\mathcal {H}_i}^{-}|^{q})^{\mathscr {X}_i})^{\frac{1}{q}})\end{array}\right\rangle .$$\end{document} To establish the validity of the statement for \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$k=l+1$$\end{document}, we start by applying the inductive hypothesis.
